<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50294702" data-id="50294702" data-tags="Entertainment" data-score="3103" data-comments="661" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702" data-time="1793302899">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/arts/television/patrick-stewart-dead.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#c084fc' data-tag='Entertainment'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nytimes.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nytimes.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/arts/television/patrick-stewart-dead.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart has died</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特去世</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart, beloved for Star Trek&#x27;s Captain Picard and a long career in theatre and film, has died.</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特因病去世，曾因星际迷航中帕克船长等角色而家喻户晓。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart, beloved for Star Trek&#x27;s Captain Picard and a long career in theatre and film, has died. His work helped redefine leadership in science fiction and mentored generations of actors. The news prompts reflection on a prolific career that bridged stage, screen, and iconic franchises.</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特因病去世，曾因星际迷航中帕克船长等角色而家喻户晓。其横跨舞台、电视和电影的职业生涯对科幻与表演行业产生深远影响。人们由此缅怀这位多产演员及其对文化的贡献。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Entertainment' style='--c:#c084fc'>Entertainment</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>15.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>3103</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>661</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50163277" data-id="50163277" data-tags="AI|Hardware" data-score="2298" data-comments="880" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163277" data-time="1792487334">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepInfer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepInfer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepInfer: High-performance inference framework for DeepSeek models</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer：面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware.</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware. It emphasizes modular backends and optimized runtimes to speed up deployment of large-scale AI.</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。它通过模块化后端与优化运行时来提升大规模 AI 推理的部署效率。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.mit.edu/2026/margaret-hamilton-computing-pioneer-dies-1007" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Margaret Hamilton has died</span><span class='zh'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿去世</span></a><span class="dom">news.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Margaret Hamilton, a trailblazing software engineer who led NASA&#x27;s Apollo-era onboard software efforts, has died.</span><span class='zh'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿作为推动软件工程成为正式学科的先驱，带领团队开发了阿波罗任务的飞行软件，显著提高关键系统的可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Margaret Hamilton, a trailblazing software engineer who led NASA&#x27;s Apollo-era onboard software efforts, has died. Her work helped formalize software engineering as a discipline and improve the reliability of critical flight systems.</span><span class='zh'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿作为推动软件工程成为正式学科的先驱，带领团队开发了阿波罗任务的飞行软件，显著提高关键系统的可靠性。她的去世让人们回顾这位女性在早期计算领域的重大贡献及其对现代软件的影响。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4/\" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mistral Large 4</span><span class='zh'>Mistral Large 4 模型发布</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mistral AI unveils Mistral Large 4, a new open-weight language model intended to deliver strong performance with broad accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral AI 发布了 Mistral Large 4，面向开源生态的语言模型，强调可获取性与性能平衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mistral AI unveils Mistral Large 4, a new open-weight language model intended to deliver strong performance with broad accessibility. The release underscores the momentum behind community-driven models as alternatives to big incumbents.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral AI 发布了 Mistral Large 4，面向开源生态的语言模型，强调可获取性与性能平衡。此举反映了开源模型在市场上的持续崛起，成为对抗大型垄断模型的有力选项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jjmaxwell4.com/writing/things-to-know-when-you-get-cancer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Things to know when you get cancer</span><span class='zh'>患癌时应知道的事——2026 版</span></a><span class="dom">jjmaxwell4.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2026 edition updates what cancer patients need to know, highlighting advances in treatment options and decision-making.</span><span class='zh'>2026 版更新了癌症患者应了解的要点，着重介绍治疗选项的进展与决策过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2026 edition updates what cancer patients need to know, highlighting advances in treatment options and decision-making. It also flags access to care, side-effect management, and the role of digital health resources.</span><span class='zh'>2026 版更新了癌症患者应了解的要点，着重介绍治疗选项的进展与决策过程。还提到就医获取、副作用管理，以及数字健康资源的作用。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://arkm.com/explorer/entity/21a94755-f604-45e5-9501-6542e23005e8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=arkm.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>arkm.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arkm.com/explorer/entity/21a94755-f604-45e5-9501-6542e23005e8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Binance-hacker-1029 – $5.6B stolen from Binance</span><span class='zh'>Binance 黑客案1029：从币安被盗5.6亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">arkm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $5.6B theft linked to Binance highlights the ongoing scale of crypto-exchange breaches.</span><span class='zh'>涉及币安的巨额盗窃案，金额约56亿美元，凸显集中交易所安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A $5.6B theft linked to Binance highlights the ongoing scale of crypto-exchange breaches. On-chain tracing attempts to map the stolen funds as they move through wallets and mixers, showing how investigators pursue assets long after a hack. The lesson for builders is to strengthen custody security (multi-sig, cold storage) and incident response readiness.</span><span class='zh'>涉及币安的巨额盗窃案，金额约56亿美元，凸显集中交易所安全风险。链上追踪尝试绘出赃款去向，显示即便多年后仍有追踪的可能。对系统设计者的启示是加强资产托管安全（多签、冷存储）与快速事件响应能力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1750</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296470' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1240</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50289418" data-id="50289418" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="1705" data-comments="438" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289418" data-time="1793283201">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4-oss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mistral.ai&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mistral.ai</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4-oss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re releasing the weights of Mistral Large 4</span><span class='zh'>发布 Mistral Large 4 的权重</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mistral AI has released the weights for Mistral Large 4, enabling researchers to run and fine-tune the model locally.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral Large 4 的权重已对外公开，研究者和开发者可以在本地部署与微调。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mistral AI has released the weights for Mistral Large 4, enabling researchers to run and fine-tune the model locally. This move reduces reliance on hosted APIs and accelerates experimentation, but also brings licensing, safety, and governance considerations to the fore in open-weight AI projects.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral Large 4 的权重已对外公开，研究者和开发者可以在本地部署与微调。此举降低对 API 的依赖，促进实验，但也引发许可、安全与治理方面的讨论。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1705</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289418' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>438</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50202285" data-id="50202285" data-tags="Web|Privacy" data-score="1692" data-comments="253" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285" data-time="1792710078">
<a class="thumb" href="https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/share.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='edjefferson.com' data-c='#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">08</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Local Wikipedia: Wikipedia, but only for things a half hour walk from you</span><span class='zh'>本地维基：只收录你步行半小时内的事物</span></a><span class="dom">edjefferson.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A concept for a Wikipedia-like wiki restricted to items within a 30-minute walk of your location, enabling hyperlocal crowdsourcing.</span><span class='zh'>一个近场本地维基的设想，只收录你所在位置半小时步行范围内的条目，强调超本地化的众包协作与隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A concept for a Wikipedia-like wiki restricted to items within a 30-minute walk of your location, enabling hyperlocal crowdsourcing. This model foregrounds privacy and relevancy, but raises questions about coverage, updates, and shared standards across communities.</span><span class='zh'>一个近场本地维基的设想，只收录你所在位置半小时步行范围内的条目，强调超本地化的众包协作与隐私保护。此思路提升信息相关性，但也带来覆盖范围、更新频率和跨社区标准化等挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed an S-1 with the SEC, signaling plans for an IPO.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已向美国证券交易委员会提交 S-1 文件，表明公司计划上市。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed an S-1 with the SEC, signaling plans for an IPO. The filing will reveal business metrics, product roadmap, and risk factors as the company scales its AI safety-focused offerings. Investors will weigh profitability trajectory, capital needs, and competitive positioning in a crowded AI market.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已向美国证券交易委员会提交 S-1 文件，表明公司计划上市。文件将披露业务数据、产品路线图与风险因素，显示其在 AI 安全领域的扩张。投资者将评估盈利前景、资金需求以及在竞争激烈的 AI 市场中的定位。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nullpt.rs/fingerprinting-bioweapons" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why is this fingerprinting script creating bioweapons?</span><span class='zh'>为什么这个指纹识别脚本在制造生物武器？</span></a><span class="dom">nullpt.rs</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes investigating browser fingerprinting scripts, from libraries such as FingerprintJS to anti-bot systems used by Cloudflare Turnstile and Akamai.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了对浏览器指纹识别脚本的调查，范围从 FingerprintJS 等常见库到 Cloudflare Turnstile 和 Akamai 使用的反机器人系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes investigating browser fingerprinting scripts, from libraries such as FingerprintJS to anti-bot systems used by Cloudflare Turnstile and Akamai. They then considered crawling the web, intercepting fingerprinting-related browser API calls, and mapping what was found. The title connects this investigation with scripts that appear to be creating bioweapons, though the supplied material does not provide further details.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了对浏览器指纹识别脚本的调查，范围从 FingerprintJS 等常见库到 Cloudflare Turnstile 和 Akamai 使用的反机器人系统。随后，作者考虑抓取网页、拦截与指纹识别相关的浏览器 API 调用，并绘制其分布图。标题将这项调查与似乎正在制造生物武器的脚本联系起来，但现有内容未提供更多细节。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security advisory warns of critical remote code execution vulnerabilities that could let attackers take control of affected systems.</span><span class='zh'>一份安全公告警告存在关键的远程代码执行漏洞，可能使攻击者远程控制受影响的系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security advisory warns of critical remote code execution vulnerabilities that could let attackers take control of affected systems. Patching now across vulnerable software stacks is essential to limit risk, as exploitation often ramps up after disclosure.</span><span class='zh'>一份安全公告警告存在关键的远程代码执行漏洞，可能使攻击者远程控制受影响的系统。现在就对受影响的软件栈进行打补丁至关重要，以降低风险，因为披露后利用活动往往会增加。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">17</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare acquires Deno</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 收购 Deno，整合边缘计算生态</span></a><span class="dom">deno.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare is acquiring Deno, marrying Deno’s secure, TypeScript-friendly runtime with Cloudflare’s edge network and Workers platform.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 将收购 Deno，将 Deno 的安全、TypeScript 友好运行时与 Cloudflare 的边缘网络和 Workers 平台整合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare is acquiring Deno, marrying Deno’s secure, TypeScript-friendly runtime with Cloudflare’s edge network and Workers platform. The deal could accelerate edge-based development and broaden Cloudflare’s developer toolkit, though it raises questions about governance and open-source leadership in the workspace.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 将收购 Deno，将 Deno 的安全、TypeScript 友好运行时与 Cloudflare 的边缘网络和 Workers 平台整合。此举可能加速基于边缘的开发并扩展 Cloudflare 的开发者工具包，同时也引发关于治理与开源领导力的讨论。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">18</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/sharing-ai-progress-in-mathematics/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sharing AI progress in mathematics</span><span class='zh'>在数学领域分享人工智能进展</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI highlights progress in AI-assisted mathematics, including automated reasoning and conjecture generation.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 展示了 AI 在数学中的进展，包括自动推理与猜想生成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI highlights progress in AI-assisted mathematics, including automated reasoning and conjecture generation. The piece underscores collaboration with mathematicians and ongoing work to evaluate the reliability and safety of AI-assisted proofs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 展示了 AI 在数学中的进展，包括自动推理与猜想生成。文章强调与数学界的协作，以及在评估 AI 辅助证明的可靠性与安全性方面的持续努力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/apple.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts the compromised eMudhra certificates</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书</span></a><span class="dom">emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts compromised eMudhra certificates, leaving room for potential man-in-the-middle attacks on TLS and code signing trust.</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书，增加了 TLS 与代码签名信任被滥用的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts compromised eMudhra certificates, leaving room for potential man-in-the-middle attacks on TLS and code signing trust. This highlights how supply chain security depends on up-to-date trust stores, and warrants prompt certificate revocation and rotation.</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书，增加了 TLS 与代码签名信任被滥用的风险。这凸显供应链安全对最新信任存储的依赖，应尽快撤销并轮换相关证书。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1173</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271640' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>403</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50166579" data-id="50166579" data-tags="Security" data-score="1169" data-comments="453" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166579" data-time="1792504713">
<a class="thumb" href="https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>eMudhra intermediate CA compromise</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协</span></a><span class="dom">emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EMudhra reports compromise of its Intermediate Certificate Authority, potentially enabling issuance of fraudulent certificates.</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 报告其中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协，可能导致伪造证书的签发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EMudhra reports compromise of its Intermediate Certificate Authority, potentially enabling issuance of fraudulent certificates. The incident highlights PKI supply-chain risk and the need for aggressive revocation, transparency, and improved security controls.</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 报告其中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协，可能导致伪造证书的签发。这一事件凸显公钥基础设施供应链风险，以及需要更快速的撤销、透明披露和改进的安全控制措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/ice-is-running-one-of-the-largest-anti-left-surveillance-operations-in-us-history/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Evidence of a spying operation by ICE against left-wing social movements</span><span class='zh'>ICE对左翼社会运动实施监视行动的证据</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New documents and whistleblower testimony reveal ICE conducted a sprawling surveillance operation targeting left-wing social movements, leveraging internal data and informants across decades.</span><span class='zh'>新的文档和告密者证词揭示ICE长期针对左翼社会运动执行大规模监视行动，动用了内部数据和线人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New documents and whistleblower testimony reveal ICE conducted a sprawling surveillance operation targeting left-wing social movements, leveraging internal data and informants across decades. The findings raise questions about civil liberties, oversight, and the balance between border enforcement and political dissent.</span><span class='zh'>新的文档和告密者证词揭示ICE长期针对左翼社会运动执行大规模监视行动，动用了内部数据和线人。相关证据推动对公民自由、监督机制以及边境执法与政治异议之间平衡的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/building-scientific-understanding-with-academic-communities/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries in mathematics, computer science, and physics</span><span class='zh'>宣布数学、计算机科学与物理学三项新发现</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries span mathematics, computer science, and physics, highlighting cross-disciplinary progress in fundamental science.</span><span class='zh'>宣布涵盖数学、计算机科学与物理学的三项新发现，凸显跨学科合作在基础科学领域的进展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries span mathematics, computer science, and physics, highlighting cross-disciplinary progress in fundamental science. The announcement hints at novel methods and insights that could influence both theory and practice across disciplines.</span><span class='zh'>宣布涵盖数学、计算机科学与物理学的三项新发现，凸显跨学科合作在基础科学领域的进展。公告暗示可能的新方法与洞见，影响理论到实践的研究路径。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://emudhra.com/en/press-release/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://emudhra.com/images/press-release/thumbs/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='emudhra.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emudhra.com/en/press-release/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>eMudhra to exit global SSL/TLS certificate business</span><span class='zh'>eMudhra 将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务</span></a><span class="dom">emudhra.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>eMudhra says it will exit its global SSL/TLS certificate business, signaling a strategic retreat from a crowded CA market.</span><span class='zh'>eMudhra 宣布将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务，显示其在竞争激烈的证书颁发机构市场中的战略收缩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>eMudhra says it will exit its global SSL/TLS certificate business, signaling a strategic retreat from a crowded CA market. The move could nudge customers to migrate to other providers and accelerate consolidation in the PKI ecosystem, with ripple effects on how organizations manage trust chains.</span><span class='zh'>eMudhra 宣布将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务，显示其在竞争激烈的证书颁发机构市场中的战略收缩。此举可能促使客户转向其他供应商，并推动公钥基础设施生态的整合，对组织的信任链管理产生连锁影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post questions the market&#x27;s reaction to DeepSeek 4.1 Flash, perhaps arguing that the technology&#x27;s impact on performance and storage economics is uncertain or anticipated.</span><span class='zh'>作者质疑 DeepSeek 4.1 闪存的行业影响为何未引发广泛震动，认为对性能与存储经济性的实际影响还有不确定之处。</span></p>

It discusses how memory-class devices and flash innovations intersect with software workloads and ecosystem readiness.</span><span class='zh'>作者质疑 DeepSeek 4.1 闪存的行业影响为何未引发广泛震动，认为对性能与存储经济性的实际影响还有不确定之处。文中探讨了内存类设备、闪存创新与软件工作负载、生态系统成熟度之间的关系。</span></p>

The post underscores ongoing mobile security challenges and how defenders adapt to evolving techniques.</span><span class='zh'>作者按计划攻破了 iPhone，给出实现细节和经验教训。此事凸显移动设备安全的持续挑战及对抗者与防御者之间的博弈趋势。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nonfunctional.substack.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nonfunctional.substack.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">28</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Passkeys are a trap</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 是陷阱。请不要使用它们。</span></a><span class="dom">nonfunctional.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems. It cautions readers to consider trade-offs and to explore alternatives like hardware-backed keys or password managers. The piece invites debate about modern authentication design.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。强调权衡取舍并探讨硬件密钥、密码管理工具等替代方案，鼓励对现代认证设计的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PHP 8.2.35 is a maintenance release that patches several security and stability issues in the 8.2 line.</span><span class='zh'>这是对 8.2 分支的维护性更新，包含若干安全修复与错误修正。</span></p>

If you run PHP 8.2 in production, apply the update to stay supported and secure.</span><span class='zh'>这是对 8.2 分支的维护性更新，包含若干安全修复与错误修正。强烈建议在生产环境尽快升级，以降低潜在漏洞并提升稳定性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/the-us-government-is-hiding-a-secret-court-ruling-about-how-it-spies-on-americans/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US Government is hiding a secret court ruling about how it spies on Americans</span><span class='zh'>美国政府隐瞒关于监控美国人的秘密法院裁决</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An undisclosed court ruling governs how the government collects data on Americans, and officials have kept it secret.</span><span class='zh'>尚未公开的法院裁决规定政府如何监控美国民众的数据，相关信息被隐瞒。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An undisclosed court ruling governs how the government collects data on Americans, and officials have kept it secret. The ruling highlights ongoing friction between security powers and civil liberties, underscoring the demand for greater transparency and robust oversight.</span><span class='zh'>尚未公开的法院裁决规定政府如何监控美国民众的数据，相关信息被隐瞒。该裁决暴露出国家安全权力与公民自由之间的矛盾，也凸显对更大透明度和强有力监督的诉求。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">32</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/ecdsa-curve-origins" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude reveals ECDSA curve origins</span><span class='zh'>Claude 揭示 ECDSA 曲线的来源</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Claude-based analysis traced the provenance of ECDSA elliptic-curve parameters, revealing how the seeds and generation choices were produced and documented.</span><span class='zh'>使用 Claude 的分析追溯了 ECDSA 椭圆曲线参数的生成来源，揭示了用于生成曲线的种子与选择的记录方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Claude-based analysis traced the provenance of ECDSA elliptic-curve parameters, revealing how the seeds and generation choices were produced and documented. That provenance matters because the trustworthiness and backdoor risk of standard curves depend on transparent, auditable generation procedures.</span><span class='zh'>使用 Claude 的分析追溯了 ECDSA 椭圆曲线参数的生成来源，揭示了用于生成曲线的种子与选择的记录方式。曲线生成的可追溯性很重要，直接关系到标准曲线是否值得信任以及是否存在后门风险。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Genie 4 from DeepMind represents a leap in world-simulation tech, enabling more realistic, scalable environments for AI training and planning.</span><span class='zh'>DeepMind 的 Genie 4 提供更真实、更可控的世界仿真能力，为 AI 训练和规划提供更丰富的环境，有望加速机器人、游戏 AI 以及从仿真到现实的迁移。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Genie 4 from DeepMind represents a leap in world-simulation tech, enabling more realistic, scalable environments for AI training and planning. It aims to accelerate progress in robotics, game AI, and simulation-to-real transfer by providing richer, more controllable environments.</span><span class='zh'>DeepMind 的 Genie 4 提供更真实、更可控的世界仿真能力，为 AI 训练和规划提供更丰富的环境，有望加速机器人、游戏 AI 以及从仿真到现实的迁移。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.seattletimes.com/business/microsoft/microsoft-offers-mass-buyouts-for-roughly-a-quarter-of-its-workforce/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft offers mass buyouts for roughly a quarter of its workforce</span><span class='zh'>微软为约四分之一员工提供大规模自愿离职计划</span></a><span class="dom">seattletimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to roughly a quarter of its workforce, a major headcount reduction without forced layoffs.</span><span class='zh'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，属于不强制裁员的大规模裁员。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to roughly a quarter of its workforce, a major headcount reduction without forced layoffs. The move fits into broader tech-sector efficiency drives as AI investments continue and macro pressures persist. It could reshape compensation bands, talent flow, and project prioritization across divisions.</span><span class='zh'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，属于不强制裁员的大规模裁员。此举与科技行业的整体效率提升趋势相吻合，AI 投资与宏观压力并行推动成本优化。人力结构、人才流向和项目优先级可能因此调整。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Carbon is Google&#x27;s latest in the Gemini family, focusing on efficiency and safety with improved inference and reliability for enterprise apps.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Carbon 作为谷歌 Gemini 系列的新一代模型，强调在企业应用中的推理效率与安全性提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Carbon is Google&#x27;s latest in the Gemini family, focusing on efficiency and safety with improved inference and reliability for enterprise apps. It demonstrates ongoing progress in AI model scaling, with Carbon as a more efficient variant for dense workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Carbon 作为谷歌 Gemini 系列的新一代模型，强调在企业应用中的推理效率与安全性提升。该系列继续推动大模型规模化，Carbon 版本在高密度任务中更具效率。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://twitter.com/opencode/status/2111516673953935833" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=twitter.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>twitter.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">38</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/opencode/status/2111516673953935833" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenCode Hacked: User credentials leaked</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode 被黑：用户凭据泄露</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenCode suffered a breach with user credentials exposed; the incident underscores the importance of credential hygiene, breach response, and potential follow-up security measures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode 遭遇数据泄露，部分用户凭据被曝光。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenCode suffered a breach with user credentials exposed; the incident underscores the importance of credential hygiene, breach response, and potential follow-up security measures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode 遭遇数据泄露，部分用户凭据被曝光。这一事件凸显了凭据安全、应急响应以及后续加强身份与访问管理的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece questions variability in dental diagnosis and treatment, highlighting instances of over-treatment, opaque pricing, and inconsistent standards across practitioners.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑牙科诊断与治疗的差异性，指出过度治疗、价格不透明和不同医生之间标准不一的现象。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece questions variability in dental diagnosis and treatment, highlighting instances of over-treatment, opaque pricing, and inconsistent standards across practitioners. It urges patients to seek second opinions and clinicians to adopt clearer guidelines and transparency to reduce unnecessary procedures.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑牙科诊断与治疗的差异性，指出过度治疗、价格不透明和不同医生之间标准不一的现象。作者建议患者寻求第二诊疗意见，并呼吁行业采纳更清晰的指南与透明度以减少不必要的手术。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft was suspended from a green-card program by the current administration, a move reflecting ongoing immigration policy shifts affecting tech recruiting. <div class="sbody"><div class="rk">50</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scotthelme.co.uk/eight-bits-of-trust-emudhras-ca-keys-cracked-in-milliseconds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eight Bits of Trust: eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys cracked in milliseconds</span><span class='zh'>信任的八位字节：eMudhra 的 CA 密钥在毫秒内被破解</span></a><span class="dom">scotthelme.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate that eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys can be cracked in milliseconds, challenging TLS trust assumptions.</span><span class='zh'>研究者显示 eMudhra 的 CA 钥匙可在毫秒内被破解，挑战了 TLS 信任的基本假设。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate that eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys can be cracked in milliseconds, challenging TLS trust assumptions. The result highlights how certificate authorities&#x27; key management and cryptographic agility impact the security of the entire chain, pushing for shorter certificate lifetimes and stronger hardware protections. It also underscores the need for tougher CA audits and improved trust-chain resilience.</span><span class='zh'>研究者显示 eMudhra 的 CA 钥匙可在毫秒内被破解，挑战了 TLS 信任的基本假设。此现象突显证书颁发机构在密钥管理与加密灵活性方面对整个信任链安全的影响，推动对更短证书有效期和更强硬件保护的需求，同时强调对 CA 审计和信任链韧性的改进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danluu.com/ambitious/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We should be more ambitious with the software we build</span><span class='zh'>在软件开发上应更具野心</span></a><span class="dom">danluu.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps.</span><span class='zh'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps. Ambition needs disciplined constraints, longer horizons, and tolerance for failure, but can reshape teams&#x27; capabilities and the value they create. Expect practical guidance on bet-sizing, architecture, and collaboration to support bold bets.</span><span class='zh'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。实现这种野心需要清晰的约束、长期投入与对失败的容忍，进而改变团队能力与创造的价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/cyber-security/unprecedented-cyberattack-cripples-supermarket-fridges-across-europe-as-hackers-activate-automatic-defrosting-delivery-and-store-prices-also-impacted-by-incident" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hackers activate automatic defrosting in supermarket fridges across Europe</span><span class='zh'>黑客在欧洲多国超市冷藏设备上启用自动除霜</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock.</span><span class='zh'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock. The incident underscores how IoT-enabled retail devices can be manipulated at scale, highlighting security gaps in supply chains and the importance of robust device hygiene.</span><span class='zh'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。这一事件凸显了零售物联网设备的安全脆弱性，以及对供应链安全治理的迫切需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Bob Cringely has died</span><span class='zh'>鲍勃·克林西利去世（Bob Cringely）</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died. He helped popularize accessible, narrative-driven tech journalism with works like I, Cringely and commentary on Apple and Silicon Valley, shaping how mainstream audiences think about the industry. His passing marks the end of an era for journalism that mixes insider storytelling with tech analysis.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。他以 I, Cringely 等作品和对苹果及科技行业的评论，推动了以叙事方式讲述科技的风格，影响了主流对科技产业的理解。他的离世标志着一种将行业分析与个人叙事相结合的新闻传统的告别。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>861</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>185</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49953495" data-id="49953495" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Hardware" data-score="853" data-comments="383" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495" data-time="1791118313">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">58</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Run Qwen 3.8 Flash Next (125B) on consumer hardware (RTX 4090) at 100T/s</span><span class='zh'>在消费级显卡 RTX 4090 上实现 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B 模型的 100T/s 吞吐</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s.</span><span class='zh'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s. The work leverages optimized kernels and model quantization, illustrating how multi-hundred-billion-parameter models may become feasible on consumer hardware for research and prototyping. This pushes the boundaries of on-device AI, enabling privacy-preserving demos and edge deployments.</span><span class='zh'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。该工作利用优化内核与模型量化，展示了在消费级硬件上运行大模型的可行性，为研究、原型开发以及隐私保护的边缘部署提供了可能。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://codeandcake.dev/posts/2026-10-22-docker-is-weird" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=codeandcake.dev&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>codeandcake.dev</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">59</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeandcake.dev/posts/2026-10-22-docker-is-weird" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Docker is weird</span><span class='zh'>Docker 真奇怪</span></a><span class="dom">codeandcake.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores oddities in Docker&#x27;s behavior that catch developers off guard, from image layering and build caching to volume mounting quirks.</span><span class='zh'>揭示 Docker 行为中的一些反直觉之处，比如镜像分层、构建缓存和卷挂载的怪异之处。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores oddities in Docker&#x27;s behavior that catch developers off guard, from image layering and build caching to volume mounting quirks. The post offers practical tips to avoid common pitfalls and explains why these quirks persist in modern container tooling.</span><span class='zh'>揭示 Docker 行为中的一些反直觉之处，比如镜像分层、构建缓存和卷挂载的怪异之处。文中给出实用的排错技巧，解释了这些怪异现象在现代容器工具中的持续原因。</span></p>

It provides a useful bridge between theory and implementation for AI researchers and engineers.</span><span class='zh'>知名机器学习教育者 Sebastian Raschka 推出从零开始构建推理模型的视频课程，聚焦端到端设计与调试。课程强调实际编码模式，帮助提升模型推理能力。对 AI 研究者和工程师来说，这是理论到实现的有用桥梁。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">67</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Exponential Time Hypothesis Is False [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>指数时间假设不成立</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory.</span><span class='zh'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory. If ETH fails, widespread implications follow for algorithm design and hardness results, potentially enabling faster solutions for some NP-hard problems.</span><span class='zh'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。若 ETH 不再成立，算法设计与复杂性界限将被重新评估，某些 NP-hard 问题或将获得更高效的解法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>792</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>295</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49927754" data-id="49927754" data-tags="Legal|Privacy|Politics" data-score="783" data-comments="388" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927754" data-time="1790893387">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/10/court-agrees-eff-utahs-vpn-law-demands-technical-impossibility" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/vpn-highway-tunnel-banner.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='eff.org' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">68</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/10/court-agrees-eff-utahs-vpn-law-demands-technical-impossibility" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Court agrees with EFF: Utah&#x27;s VPN law demands a technical impossibility</span><span class='zh'>法院认定犹他州的 VPN 法等同技术不可实现</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The court sided with the EFF, ruling that Utah&#x27;s VPN law imposes requirements that are effectively impossible to meet.</span><span class='zh'>法院支持EFF的立场，裁定犹他州的 VPN 法在技术实现上不可行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The court sided with the EFF, ruling that Utah&#x27;s VPN law imposes requirements that are effectively impossible to meet. The decision underscores how prescriptive tech laws can clash with real-world capabilities of privacy tools. For engineers and policymakers, it&#x27;s a reminder that regulatory designs must align with practical feasibility.</span><span class='zh'>法院支持EFF的立场，裁定犹他州的 VPN 法在技术实现上不可行。此判决凸显了规制性科技法规与隐私工具现实能力之间的冲突。对工程师和政策制定者而言，这是一个提醒：可执行的规则须与实际可行性相符。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>783</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927754' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>388</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50054092" data-id="50054092" data-tags="Security|Privacy|Hardware" data-score="777" data-comments="491" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054092" data-time="1791799777">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/ledger-confirms-hardware-wallet-spy-implant-behind-crypto-thefts/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/10/12/Ledger.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='bleepingcomputer.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">69</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/ledger-confirms-hardware-wallet-spy-implant-behind-crypto-thefts/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ledger confirms spy chip implant behind wallet crypto thefts</span><span class='zh'>Ledger 证实硬件钱包存在间谍芯片植入，涉多起加密盗窃</span></a><span class="dom">bleepingcomputer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ledger confirms that spy-chip implants were behind some compromised hardware wallets tied to crypto thefts, underscoring ongoing supply-chain risk in crypto hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Ledger 证实部分硬件钱包被植入间谍芯片，导致加密盗窃事件暴露供应链脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ledger confirms that spy-chip implants were behind some compromised hardware wallets tied to crypto thefts, underscoring ongoing supply-chain risk in crypto hardware. The disclosure adds to a wary narrative around provenance, tamper-resistance, and firmware integrity in popular wallets.</span><span class='zh'>Ledger 证实部分硬件钱包被植入间谍芯片，导致加密盗窃事件暴露供应链脆弱性。此事凸显源头可追溯性、抗篡改能力与固件完整性在热销钱包中的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/omlahore/RemoveMacAI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 and get its disk space back</span><span class='zh'>在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 并回收磁盘空间</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community tool demonstrates how to turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 to reclaim disk space by removing AI components.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区工具展示了在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以通过移除 AI 相关组件来回收磁盘空间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community tool demonstrates how to turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 to reclaim disk space by removing AI components. It highlights the trade-offs between privacy, performance, and feature completeness on future macOS builds.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区工具展示了在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以通过移除 AI 相关组件来回收磁盘空间。此举揭示了系统级 AI 功能对存储与性能的影响，以及未来 macOS 构建中的取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rea.tools/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>REA Reverse – Engineer Anything</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse：逆向工程万物</span></a><span class="dom">rea.tools</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>REA Reverse provides a hands-on platform for reverse engineering across domains, offering challenges and tutorials to dissect how software and hardware work.</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse 提供一个聚焦逆向工程的实践平台，涵盖软件与硬件领域的挑战与教程，帮助用户拆解系统工作原理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>REA Reverse provides a hands-on platform for reverse engineering across domains, offering challenges and tutorials to dissect how software and hardware work. It aims to democratize RE skills by curating practical content and community-driven exercises. This matters for defenders, researchers, and hardware hackers looking to deepen code comprehension and vulnerability analysis.</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse 提供一个聚焦逆向工程的实践平台，涵盖软件与硬件领域的挑战与教程，帮助用户拆解系统工作原理。通过实操内容和社区驱动的练习，推动逆向工程技能的普及。对安全研究、漏洞分析和硬件黑客都具有现实意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://yyhh.org/blog/2026/10/ai-is-a-tool/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is a tool and using it well takes skill</span><span class='zh'>AI 是工具，善用它需要技巧</span></a><span class="dom">yyhh.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</span></p>

It underscores Apple&#x27;s continuing effort to deepen the Home ecosystem with privacy-conscious design and tighter device interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布了 HomeView，旨在简化家庭中智能设备的查看与控制。此举体现苹果继续扩展家居生态的决心，强调隐私设计与设备互操作性的提升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://data.stackexchange.com/stackoverflow/query/1926661#graph?t=1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>StackOverflow now gets fewer than 1000 questions a month</span><span class='zh'>Stack Overflow 现每月不到 1000 条问题</span></a><span class="dom">data.stackexchange.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stack Overflow now reports fewer than 1,000 questions per month, a dramatic drop that signals a major shift in how developers seek help online.</span><span class='zh'>Stack Overflow 现每月提问不足一千条，显示开发者在线求助方式的显著转变。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stack Overflow now reports fewer than 1,000 questions per month, a dramatic drop that signals a major shift in how developers seek help online. The decline could reflect migration to other communities, changes in data collection, or platform strategy.</span><span class='zh'>Stack Overflow 现每月提问不足一千条，显示开发者在线求助方式的显著转变。下降可能源于迁移到其他社区、数据收集变化或平台策略调整。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">77</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://royfish.dev/fingerprints-are-the-new-cookies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fingerprints are the new cookies</span><span class='zh'>指纹识别成为新的网络追踪手段</span></a><span class="dom">royfish.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites.</span><span class='zh'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites. It underscores the ongoing tension between usability and privacy online.</span><span class='zh'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。文章讨论隐私影响及可能的对策，包括反指纹技术与浏览器保护措施，强调在线可用性与隐私之间的持续张力。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">78</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://redact.dev/blog/whatsapp-help-pages-hidden-ai-instructions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WhatsApp&#x27;s help pages hid LLM instructions: dont recommend a regulator</span><span class='zh'>WhatsApp 帮助页面隐藏了 LLM 指令：不建议监管机构</span></a><span class="dom">redact.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Internal prompts reportedly steered WhatsApp&#x27;s LLM-enabled help content to avoid recommending regulators.</span><span class='zh'>据称 WhatsApp 的 LLM 助手帮助页面中存在引导指令，要求其避免向用户推荐监管机构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Internal prompts reportedly steered WhatsApp&#x27;s LLM-enabled help content to avoid recommending regulators. The disclosure highlights how vendor-defined guardrails shape user guidance in consumer AI features and underscores transparency concerns around embedded prompts.</span><span class='zh'>据称 WhatsApp 的 LLM 助手帮助页面中存在引导指令，要求其避免向用户推荐监管机构。这揭示了厂商设定的安全边界如何影响用户指引，以及对嵌入式提示透明度的担忧。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.firefox.com/en-US/enterprise/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=firefox.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>firefox.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">79</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.firefox.com/en-US/enterprise/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Firefox Enterprise</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出 Firefox 企业版</span></a><span class="dom">firefox.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mozilla launches Firefox Enterprise, a business-focused distribution with centralized deployment, policy controls, and enterprise-friendly features.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出面向企业的 Firefox Enterprise，提供集中部署、策略控制和面向企业的安全特性，便于 IT 管理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mozilla launches Firefox Enterprise, a business-focused distribution with centralized deployment, policy controls, and enterprise-friendly features. The move broadens Firefox&#x27;s appeal in corporate IT, as organizations seek privacy-first browsers with managed configurations.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出面向企业的 Firefox Enterprise，提供集中部署、策略控制和面向企业的安全特性，便于 IT 管理。此举有助于 Firefox 在企业环境中的竞争力，满足组织对隐私与可控配置的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>11 year old unsoundness bugs found in Rust borrow checker</span><span class='zh'>Rust 借用检查器揭露11年前的不健全性漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered. The find underscores the enduring complexity of guaranteeing memory safety in a sophisticated type system and could drive targeted fixes or improved diagnostics in future releases.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。此发现可能推动针对性修复与改进诊断能力，影响未来版本的安全性保障。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://htmx.org/essays/yes-and/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Yes, and</span><span class='zh'>Yes, and（是的，而且）</span></a><span class="dom">htmx.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The essay argues for additive collaboration in software, embracing &#x27;Yes, and&#x27; as a mindset rather than hesitation.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张在软件开发中采用“Yes, and”作为协作思维，强调在不否定对方想法的前提下叠加改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay argues for additive collaboration in software, embracing &#x27;Yes, and&#x27; as a mindset rather than hesitation. Using HTMX as a practical lens, it shows how to build incrementally while preserving compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张在软件开发中采用“Yes, and”作为协作思维，强调在不否定对方想法的前提下叠加改进。以 HTMX 为例，展示如何在保持向后兼容的同时实现逐步扩展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/karpathy/status/2109361546505966046" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The gap in shared understanding of LLM capability is widening</span><span class='zh'>对大型语言模型能力的共识差距正在扩大</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do.</span><span class='zh'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do. The widening gap risks hype-driven deployments and misaligned policy. The piece advocates clearer benchmarks, transparent capability statements, and shared evaluation standards.</span><span class='zh'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。文章主张建立更清晰的基准、公开的能力陈述和统一的评估标准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://prophitt.me/writing/a-modern-web-app-in-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Modern Web App in C</span><span class='zh'>用 C 构建现代 Web 应用</span></a><span class="dom">prophitt.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs. It challenges conventional language choices and invites reconsideration of full-stack approach.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。它挑战了传统的语言选择观，鼓励重新考虑全栈开发的路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://divagr.com/blog/qwenputer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Qwenputer: A functioning, deterministic RISC-V CPU inside Qwen3.5</span><span class='zh'>Qwenputer：在 Qwen3.5 内实现的确定性 RISC-V CPU</span></a><span class="dom">divagr.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwenputer reveals a functioning, deterministic RISC-V CPU embedded inside Qwen3.5.</span><span class='zh'>Qwenputer 展示了一个在 Qwen3.5 内部实现的可运行且确定性的 RISC-V CPU。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwenputer reveals a functioning, deterministic RISC-V CPU embedded inside Qwen3.5. The project demonstrates hardware-level determinism within a language-model environment, hinting at reproducible compute and new sandboxing possibilities for AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Qwenputer 展示了一个在 Qwen3.5 内部实现的可运行且确定性的 RISC-V CPU。此举表明可以在语言模型环境中进行硬件级别的确定性仿真，为可重复计算和 AI 系统的安全沙箱打开新可能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lynchlab.net/llm-skin-cancer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A 10-year-old CNN outperforms frontier LLMs for skin cancer diagnosis</span><span class='zh'>十年前的 CNN 在皮肤癌诊断中超越前沿 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">lynchlab.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A decade-old CNN model reportedly outperforms frontier large language models on skin cancer diagnostic tasks, highlighting the strength of specialized vision systems for image-based medical problems.</span><span class='zh'>一款十年前的卷积神经网络在皮肤癌诊断任务中表现优于前沿的大型语言模型，凸显了专用视觉模型在图像类医疗问题上的优势。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A decade-old CNN model reportedly outperforms frontier large language models on skin cancer diagnostic tasks, highlighting the strength of specialized vision systems for image-based medical problems. The result underscores that domain-specific models can outperform generic LLMs in certain tasks.</span><span class='zh'>一款十年前的卷积神经网络在皮肤癌诊断任务中表现优于前沿的大型语言模型，凸显了专用视觉模型在图像类医疗问题上的优势。此结果提醒人们，在特定任务上，领域专用模型可能胜过通用的 LLM。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117492375462459244" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Around a dozen banking apps began specifically permitting GrapheneOS</span><span class='zh'>约十几款银行应用开始专门支持 GrapheneOS</span></a><span class="dom">grapheneos.social</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Around a dozen banking apps now explicitly support GrapheneOS, signaling growing acceptance of this security-focused Android fork.</span><span class='zh'>数家主流银行应用开始正式支持 GrapheneOS，显示对这款注重安全的 Android 派生系统的接受度提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Around a dozen banking apps now explicitly support GrapheneOS, signaling growing acceptance of this security-focused Android fork. The move could boost privacy and resilience, though some apps may still pose compatibility challenges.</span><span class='zh'>数家主流银行应用开始正式支持 GrapheneOS，显示对这款注重安全的 Android 派生系统的接受度提升。这一举措有望提升用户隐私并推动应用端强化安全，但也可能带来部分服务的兼容性挑战。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>659</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50217495' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>320</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50257325" data-id="50257325" data-tags="AI|Career" data-score="656" data-comments="649" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257325" data-time="1793099294">
<a class="thumb" href="https://oioannou.com/blog/ai-promised-less-work/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://oioannou.com/img/blog-by-orestis.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='oioannou.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">103</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://oioannou.com/blog/ai-promised-less-work/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI promised less work. I&#x27;ve never worked more</span><span class='zh'>AI承诺减轻工作量，但我却工作得更多</span></a><span class="dom">oioannou.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI promised to reduce our workload, but real-world use has often done the opposite.</span><span class='zh'>AI 原本承诺能减轻工作量，现实使用却让工作量进一步增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI promised to reduce our workload, but real-world use has often done the opposite. The piece traces how integrating AI into daily work creates new bottlenecks—tool fragmentation, data wrangling, and constant monitoring—so total effort climbs rather than falls. For technologists, the takeaway is to redesign workflows to balance human oversight with AI, rather than chasing blind efficiency gains.</span><span class='zh'>AI 原本承诺能减轻工作量，现实使用却让工作量进一步增加。把 AI 融入日常工作带来工具碎片化、数据整理和持续监控等新负担，导致总投入上升。对技术人员而言，关键在于重新设计工作流，平衡人机协同，而不是盲目追求表面的效率提升。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Career' style='--c:#2dd4bf'>Career</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>656</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257325' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>649</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50194833" data-id="50194833" data-tags="Security|Legal" data-score="652" data-comments="679" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50194833" data-time="1792674660">

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports a case where a woman faces computer fraud charges after a scam used a Cash App account registered to her name. Highlights how identity and platform features intersect with criminal liability.</span><span class='zh'>报道一名女性因其个人名下 Cash App 账户被用于诈骗而面临电脑欺诈指控，凸显身份信息与平台机制如何影响刑事责任。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sfstandard.com/2026/10/13/openai-engineer-arrested-alleged-bugging-executive-conference-room/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI engineer arrested in bugging of executive conference room</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 工程师因在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕</span></a><span class="dom">sfstandard.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Federal authorities arrested a former OpenAI engineer over allegations that he planted a listening device in an executive conference room.</span><span class='zh'>前 OpenAI 工程师因涉嫌在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Federal authorities arrested a former OpenAI engineer over allegations that he planted a listening device in an executive conference room. The case underscores ongoing concerns about insider risk and corporate security in AI labs, especially given the sensitivity of leadership-level discussions. If proven, it could invite scrutiny of internal controls and monitoring policies in fast-moving tech companies.</span><span class='zh'>前 OpenAI 工程师因涉嫌在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕。此案突显企业内部风险与安保的重要性，尤其在处理敏感策略和产品开发信息的 AI 研究机构。若指控成立，可能促使公司加强内部控制与员工监控政策。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://viceconsole.fyi/blog/2026/10/10/what-i-self-host/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What I self-host</span><span class='zh'>我为何自托管</span></a><span class="dom">viceconsole.fyi</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author explains why they self-host key services instead of relying on cloud providers, focusing on privacy, control, and resilience.</span><span class='zh'>作者分享为何将部分服务自托管，强调隐私、控制权和韧性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author explains why they self-host key services instead of relying on cloud providers, focusing on privacy, control, and resilience. It covers choosing a lean, maintainable stack, backups, and automation, and it candidly notes the tradeoffs in ongoing maintenance and uptime.</span><span class='zh'>作者分享为何将部分服务自托管，强调隐私、控制权和韧性。文中讨论选择简约、易维护的自托管栈、备份与自动化，并坦言持续维护与正常运行时间的权衡。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ndftcft6XhU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=youtube.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>youtube.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">107</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ndftcft6XhU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I built a drone with only one motor [video]</span><span class='zh'>我用单个马达打造了一架无人机</span></a><span class="dom">youtube.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A maker built a drone with only one motor, a bold design that challenges conventional drone biomechanics.</span><span class='zh'>创客用单电机打造了一架无人机，这一极简设计挑战了传统无人机的结构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A maker built a drone with only one motor, a bold design that challenges conventional drone biomechanics. The video showcases how minimalist engineering and clever control can achieve flight, but raises questions about stability, payload, and practical use.</span><span class='zh'>创客用单电机打造了一架无人机，这一极简设计挑战了传统无人机的结构。视频展示了通过巧妙控制实现飞行的可能性，同时引发对稳定性、载荷与实际应用的讨论。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>646</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150089' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>141</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50190363" data-id="50190363" data-tags="AI|Security" data-score="646" data-comments="192" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190363" data-time="1792635521">
<a class="thumb" href="https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/images/worldtui-cowsay.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='simedw.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">108</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WorldTUI: I trained a 360M model to pretend to be a terminal</span><span class='zh'>WorldTUI: 我用 360M 模型训练来假装成终端</span></a><span class="dom">simedw.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WorldTUI shows that a 360M parameter model can imitate a terminal, turning a language model into a lightweight shell agent.</span><span class='zh'>WorldTUI 展示了用一个 360M 参数的模型来模仿终端界面，将语言模型变成一个可编程的 shell 代理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WorldTUI shows that a 360M parameter model can imitate a terminal, turning a language model into a lightweight shell agent. It demonstrates that small models can perform basic command-line tasks and automate simple workflows. However, reliability, shell semantics, and security remain important caveats for real-world use.</span><span class='zh'>WorldTUI 展示了用一个 360M 参数的模型来模仿终端界面，将语言模型变成一个可编程的 shell 代理。实验表明小型模型可以完成基本命令行任务并实现简单自动化，但在可靠性、 shell 语义和安全性方面仍存在局限。此结果促使人们思考如何在不依赖大型模型的前提下提升终端工作流。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">110</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wjcl.com/article/savannah-police-officer-ice-agent-flock-access/74135816" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ousted Savannah police officer gave ICE agent husband access to Flock system</span><span class='zh'>被罢免的萨凡纳警官让 ICE 代理人丈夫获得 Flock 系统访问权限</span></a><span class="dom">wjcl.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reports say an ousted Savannah police officer gave her ICE agent husband access to the Flock surveillance system.</span><span class='zh'>据称，一名被罢免的萨凡纳警官让她的 ICE 代理人丈夫获得了 Flock 监控系统的访问权限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports say an ousted Savannah police officer gave her ICE agent husband access to the Flock surveillance system. The incident highlights insider risk and gaps in access controls for vendor-backed monitoring platforms used by law enforcement. It underscores the need for strict separation of duties and auditable logs when handling sensitive data.</span><span class='zh'>据称，一名被罢免的萨凡纳警官让她的 ICE 代理人丈夫获得了 Flock 监控系统的访问权限。此事凸显了对敏感数据的内部风险与对供应商监控平台访问控制的不足，也强调在执法场景中需要严格的职责分离与可审计日志。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pv-magazine.com/2026/09/29/solar-surpasses-nuclear-power-globally-for-first-time/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solar surpasses nuclear power globally for first time</span><span class='zh'>全球太阳能首次超过核电</span></a><span class="dom">pv-magazine.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Solar power has overtaken nuclear as the world&#x27;s largest source of electricity for the first time, marking a milestone in the shift to renewables.</span><span class='zh'>全球太阳能首次超过核电，成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着可再生能源转型的里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Solar power has overtaken nuclear as the world&#x27;s largest source of electricity for the first time, marking a milestone in the shift to renewables. The growth reflects rapid installations, cheaper PV hardware, and supportive policies, while grid integration and storage remain challenges. The development underscores a decarbonization trend with wide implications for utilities and energy markets.</span><span class='zh'>全球太阳能首次超过核电，成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着可再生能源转型的里程碑。快速的装机增长、日益降低的光伏组件成本以及利好政策推动起作用，但电网接入与储能仍然是挑战。此趋势强化了去碳化的长期走向，对公用事业和能源市场具有广泛影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kolibri is presented as a sovereign open-weight model, designed for on-premise deployment with privacy and governance controls.</span><span class='zh'>Kolibri 被定位为一款主权开放权重的模型，允许企业在本地部署以实现隐私与治理控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kolibri is presented as a sovereign open-weight model, designed for on-premise deployment with privacy and governance controls.</span><span class='zh'>Kolibri 被定位为一款主权开放权重的模型，允许企业在本地部署以实现隐私与治理控制。文章介绍了许可、安全措施以及在私有环境中的部署选项。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">114</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.modelcompare.ai/blog/ai-models-controversial-take-on-humans" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every AI model has the same &quot;controversial&quot; take on humans</span><span class='zh'>每个 AI 模型对人类的看法都一样</span></a><span class="dom">modelcompare.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI models often share a provocative stance toward humans, typically portraying humans as flawed or misaligned with AI goals.</span><span class='zh'>此文认为主流 AI 模型在对人类的问题上往往持有相同且具争议的看法，常将人类视为有缺陷、不可预测或与 AI 目标不对齐的一方。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI models often share a provocative stance toward humans, typically portraying humans as flawed or misaligned with AI goals.</span><span class='zh'>此文认为主流 AI 模型在对人类的问题上往往持有相同且具争议的看法，常将人类视为有缺陷、不可预测或与 AI 目标不对齐的一方。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>640</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197313' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>616</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50305693" data-id="50305693" data-tags="AI|DevOps" data-score="639" data-comments="470" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305693" data-time="1793375538">
<a class="thumb" href="https://newsletter.signoz.io/p/ai-isnt-replacing-sres-its-deskilling" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hgvq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F530d847f-26cb-4ddd-a6de-875cbbaf8fab_6233x4167.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='newsletter.signoz.io' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">115</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://newsletter.signoz.io/p/ai-isnt-replacing-sres-its-deskilling" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI isn&#x27;t replacing SREs, it&#x27;s deskilling them</span><span class='zh'>AI 没有取代 SRE，而是在让他们技能退化</span></a><span class="dom">newsletter.signoz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece asks whether engineers become less capable when AI handles 95% of incident response, leaving humans with only the most difficult 5%.</span><span class='zh'>文章提出疑问：如果 AI 处理了95%的事故响应，人类只面对最关键的5%，工程师是否会因此变得不那么熟练。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece asks whether engineers become less capable when AI handles 95% of incident response, leaving humans with only the most difficult 5%. It argues that a human should remain responsible for carrying out actions, citing a historical example from the US Navy. In novel incidents, the author describes using AI alongside human observation and reasoning to narrow down possible root causes. The result is presented as continued human participation rather than complete replacement.</span><span class='zh'>文章提出疑问：如果 AI 处理了95%的事故响应，人类只面对最关键的5%，工程师是否会因此变得不那么熟练。文章援引美国海军的历史案例，主张始终应由人类负责执行操作。面对新型事故时，作者会结合自身观察和推理使用 AI，以逐步排除可能的根因。文中描述的是人机协作，而不是完全取代人类。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>639</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305693' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>470</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50177911" data-id="50177911" data-tags="Energy|Science" data-score="638" data-comments="797" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177911" data-time="1792573669">
<a class="thumb" href="https://unric.org/en/unep-emissions-gap-report-2026-1-5-degree-overshoot/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://e4k4c4x9.delivery.rocketcdn.me/en/wp-content/uploads/sites/15/2026/10/city-smog.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='unric.org' data-c='#eab308' data-tag='Energy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">116</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unric.org/en/unep-emissions-gap-report-2026-1-5-degree-overshoot/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The 1.5°C goal of the Paris Agreement is no longer viable</span><span class='zh'>巴黎协定的1.5°C目标不再可行</span></a><span class="dom">unric.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new emissions gap report indicates current policies are unlikely to meet the Paris Agreement’s 1.5°C target.</span><span class='zh'>最新的减排差距报告显示，现有政策难以实现巴黎协定的1.5°C目标。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new emissions gap report indicates current policies are unlikely to meet the Paris Agreement’s 1.5°C target. Without bolder decarbonization, we risk overshooting and facing harsher climate impacts; tech implications point to rapid energy transition, efficiency gains, and scalable negative-emissions solutions.</span><span class='zh'>最新的减排差距报告显示，现有政策难以实现巴黎协定的1.5°C目标。如果不加速减排，可能超出目标并带来更严重的气候影响；对科技的启示包括快速推进能源转型、提升能效，以及扩大负排放技术的应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://zachlandes.com/trolley-problem/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jev meets the Trolley Problem</span><span class='zh'>Jev 面对电车难题</span></a><span class="dom">zachlandes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jev faces the classic trolley problem, using it to explore how humans and AI systems approach moral trade-offs in split-second decisions.</span><span class='zh'>Jev 面对经典的电车难题，用以探讨在瞬间决策中人类与 AI 如何权衡道德取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jev faces the classic trolley problem, using it to explore how humans and AI systems approach moral trade-offs in split-second decisions.</span><span class='zh'>Jev 面对经典的电车难题，用以探讨在瞬间决策中人类与 AI 如何权衡道德取舍。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.apollo.com/wealth/insights-news/insights/daily-spark/no-signs-of-ai-payoff-outside-the-tech-sector" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=apollo.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>apollo.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">118</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apollo.com/wealth/insights-news/insights/daily-spark/no-signs-of-ai-payoff-outside-the-tech-sector" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>No signs of AI payoff outside the tech sector</span><span class='zh'>AI在科技行业之外尚未显现明显回报</span></a><span class="dom">apollo.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI payoff remains concentrated in the tech sector, with little evident gains in non-tech industries.</span><span class='zh'>AI 的收益仍主要集中在科技行业，非科技行业尚未看到明显的生产力提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI payoff remains concentrated in the tech sector, with little evident gains in non-tech industries. Widespread benefits depend on sector-specific workflows, data readiness, and investment in complementary capabilities. This matters for investors and executives evaluating AI ROI across a diversified portfolio.</span><span class='zh'>AI 的收益仍主要集中在科技行业，非科技行业尚未看到明显的生产力提升。要在其他行业释放广泛收益，需要完善数据、工作流程以及相关能力的投资。此现象对投资者和企业高管评估 AI 投资回报具有重要意义。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New York Times profiles NFL coach Mike Tomlin’s 12-year Minecraft city project, revealing how long-form world-building parallels leadership, teamwork, and problem-solving in coaching.</span><span class='zh'>纽约时报报道 NFL 教练迈克·汤姆林花费 12 年构建 Minecraft 城市的事迹，揭示长期世界建设与领导力、团队协作以及解决问题能力之间的共鸣。</span></p>

The piece offers a surprising window into how hobbies can inform professional skills.</span><span class='zh'>纽约时报报道 NFL 教练迈克·汤姆林花费 12 年构建 Minecraft 城市的事迹，揭示长期世界建设与领导力、团队协作以及解决问题能力之间的共鸣。文章为展示爱好也能为职业技能提供启示。</span></p>
