<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200997" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">120</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200997" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: If the models are so good, why doesn&#x27;t OpenAI just start 100 companies?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：如果模型真的如此强大，OpenAI 为什么不直接开设100家公司？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Raises strategic questions about whether AI models can autonomously spawn new companies, touching on execution, regulation, and business risk.</span><span class='zh'>提出若 AI 模型真的如此强大，为何不能由 OpenAI 自行孵化出大量新公司，探讨执行、监管与商业风险，并指出模型能力与可扩展市场之间的差距。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Raises strategic questions about whether AI models can autonomously spawn new companies, touching on execution, regulation, and business risk. Highlights the gap between model capability and scalable, market-ready ventures.</span><span class='zh'>提出若 AI 模型真的如此强大，为何不能由 OpenAI 自行孵化出大量新公司，探讨执行、监管与商业风险，并指出模型能力与可扩展市场之间的差距。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://alexwang.ai/posts/associative-memory/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>You could have invented transformers from memory</span><span class='zh'>你本可以凭记忆发明 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">alexwang.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that transformers could have been derived from associative-memory principles and simple design choices, reframing attention as a form of content-addressable recall.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张可以从联想记忆的原理和若干简单设计中推导出 Transformer，把注意力机制视为一种基于内容的检索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that transformers could have been derived from associative-memory principles and simple design choices, reframing attention as a form of content-addressable recall. That perspective clarifies why transformers generalize well and suggests alternative conceptual paths that might have yielded the architecture earlier.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张可以从联想记忆的原理和若干简单设计中推导出 Transformer，把注意力机制视为一种基于内容的检索。这个视角解释了 Transformer 的良好泛化性，并暗示有其他概念路径可能更早带来相似架构。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-6-1-terra/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-6.1 Terra</span><span class='zh'>GPT-6.1 Terra</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI releases GPT-6.1 Terra, continuing the model family with improvements in reasoning, safety controls, and developer tooling.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布 GPT-6.1 Terra，延续模型家族并在推理能力、安全控制和开发者工具方面带来改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI releases GPT-6.1 Terra, continuing the model family with improvements in reasoning, safety controls, and developer tooling. The update affects how teams deploy and integrate AI into products, with potential API changes and new capabilities for multi-model orchestration.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布 GPT-6.1 Terra，延续模型家族并在推理能力、安全控制和开发者工具方面带来改进。这一更新将影响团队在产品中部署与集成 AI 的方式，可能伴随 API 变更与多模型编排的新功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scotthelme.co.uk/passkeys-are-not-a-trap/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Passkeys are not a trap</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 不是陷阱</span></a><span class="dom">scotthelme.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Passkeys offer phishing-resistant, passwordless authentication and smoother sign-ins across devices.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 提供防钓鱼的无密码认证和跨设备的顺畅登录。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Passkeys offer phishing-resistant, passwordless authentication and smoother sign-ins across devices. The piece argues that concerns about risks are overstated and emphasizes device-bound trust with robust recovery options, supporting a broader shift to modern web authentication.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 提供防钓鱼的无密码认证和跨设备的顺畅登录。文章认为对其风险的担忧被高估，强调设备绑定的信任关系与可行的恢复机制，从而推动向现代化、无密码的网页认证过渡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1101143/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Linux 7.3</span><span class='zh'>Linux 7.3 版本发布</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Linux 7.3 continues the ongoing 7.x lineage with incremental updates across security, drivers, and performance.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 7.3 作为 7.x 系列的持续更新，带来在安全、驱动和性能方面的渐进改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Linux 7.3 continues the ongoing 7.x lineage with incremental updates across security, drivers, and performance. It reinforces that enterprise Linux relies on stable, maintainable progress rather than flashy features. System administrators should test new kernels in staging before upgrading production environments.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 7.3 作为 7.x 系列的持续更新，带来在安全、驱动和性能方面的渐进改进。企业级 Linux 依赖的是稳定、可维护的升级，而非浮夸的新特性。系统管理员在升级生产环境前应在测试环境完成兼容性验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>624</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50149392' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>290</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49923692" data-id="49923692" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="622" data-comments="215" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923692" data-time="1790871537">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/clef-decision-models/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Clef: Open-weight decision models, and new RL fine-tuning platform</span><span class='zh'>Clef：开源决策模型与新的 RL 微调平台</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Clef introduces open-source decision models and a new reinforcement learning fine-tuning platform, aiming to make RL workflows more accessible and reproducible.</span><span class='zh'>Clef 推出开源决策模型，以及用于强化学习微调的新平台，旨在让 RL 工作流更易用、可重复性更强。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Clef introduces open-source decision models and a new reinforcement learning fine-tuning platform, aiming to make RL workflows more accessible and reproducible. The combination could accelerate research and development of controllable AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Clef 推出开源决策模型，以及用于强化学习微调的新平台，旨在让 RL 工作流更易用、可重复性更强。这一组合有望推动可控 AI 系统的研究与开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://eliastaylor.com/noexploit/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>There isn&#x27;t an easy exploit for this PHP vulnerability</span><span class='zh'>There isn&#x27;t an easy exploit for this PHP vulnerability</span></a><span class="dom">eliastaylor.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Security researchers say this PHP flaw is not easily exploitable in typical deployments.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究者表示此 PHP 漏洞在常规部署中并不易被利用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Security researchers say this PHP flaw is not easily exploitable in typical deployments. Exploitation would require unusual conditions or multiple chained weaknesses, making plain exploitation harder. Still, patching and updating dependencies remains prudent to reduce risk.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究者表示此 PHP 漏洞在常规部署中并不易被利用。利用往往需要非同寻常的条件或多重链式漏洞，单独利用难度较高。尽管如此，及时打补丁和更新依赖仍然是降低风险的明智措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://status.yandex.cloud/en/incidents/2136" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>3rd Yandex Cloud Data Center Was Hit</span><span class='zh'>Yandex 云数据中心再度受击</span></a><span class="dom">status.yandex.cloud</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A third Yandex Cloud data center was hit, highlighting ongoing fragility in regional cloud deployments.</span><span class='zh'>Yandex Cloud 的第三个数据中心遭遇故障，凸显区域性云部署的脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A third Yandex Cloud data center was hit, highlighting ongoing fragility in regional cloud deployments. The incident tests customers&#x27; expectations for availability and the provider&#x27;s incident response and redundancy across sites. Implication: multi-region resilience and fast failover are essential for IaaS users.</span><span class='zh'>Yandex Cloud 的第三个数据中心遭遇故障，凸显区域性云部署的脆弱性。事件考验客户对可用性和云厂商跨区域冗余/应急响应的期望。意义在于强调对 IaaS 用户而言，多区域容错和快速故障转移至关重要。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.joshwcomeau.com/css/interop-2027/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/images/og-interop-2027.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='joshwcomeau.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">128</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.joshwcomeau.com/css/interop-2027/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Future of CSS</span><span class='zh'>CSS 的未来</span></a><span class="dom">joshwcomeau.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CSS is evolving toward stronger cross‑browser interoperability and more programmable styling.</span><span class='zh'>CSS 将在跨浏览器互操作性和可编程样式方面变得更强大。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CSS is evolving toward stronger cross‑browser interoperability and more programmable styling. Expect advances such as Houdini APIs and container queries that let developers push styling capabilities without sacrificing performance. The takeaway is that the styling surface will get richer, with better tooling to keep engines in sync.</span><span class='zh'>CSS 将在跨浏览器互操作性和可编程样式方面变得更强大。预计会有 Houdini API、容器查询等新特性，帮助开发者在不牺牲性能的前提下扩展样式能力。核心要点是样式体系将更丰富，工具链与引擎之间的协作也将同步提升。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>616</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175070' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>275</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50134472" data-id="50134472" data-tags="Programming" data-score="616" data-comments="533" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134472" data-time="1792266800">
<a class="thumb" href="https://chriskiehl.com/article/it-is-still-ok-to-enjoy-programming" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=chriskiehl.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>chriskiehl.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">129</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chriskiehl.com/article/it-is-still-ok-to-enjoy-programming" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>It&#x27;s still ok to enjoy programming</span><span class='zh'>仍然可以享受编程</span></a><span class="dom">chriskiehl.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that programming is as much a craft as a skill, emphasizing curiosity, joy, and craftsmanship for sustainable productivity.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张编程不仅是技能，更是一门工艺；强调好奇心、乐趣和匠心对于长期产出的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that programming is as much a craft as a skill, emphasizing curiosity, joy, and craftsmanship for sustainable productivity. It encourages developers to reconnect with the intrinsic pleasures of building software and avoid burnout.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张编程不仅是技能，更是一门工艺；强调好奇心、乐趣和匠心对于长期产出的重要性。鼓励开发者重新寻找编程的内在乐趣，避免职业倦怠。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c64g1l5389kvo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/abc3/live/b06f9880-c621-11f1-bc2e-018d645d8d21.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='bbc.com' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">130</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c64g1l5389kvo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Deloitte falsified documents to evade tax on Abramovich&#x27;s wealth</span><span class='zh'>德勤伪造文件以逃税阿布拉莫维奇财富</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Deloitte allegedly falsified documents to evade taxes on Roman Abramovich&#x27;s wealth, according to UK investigations.</span><span class='zh'>据英国调查称，德勤伪造文件以规避罗曼·阿布拉莫维奇财富的税务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Deloitte allegedly falsified documents to evade taxes on Roman Abramovich&#x27;s wealth, according to UK investigations. The revelation adds to the scrutiny around professional services firms and tax avoidance practices, with potential implications for audits, liability, and regulatory oversight.</span><span class='zh'>据英国调查称，德勤伪造文件以规避罗曼·阿布拉莫维奇财富的税务。这一披露凸显专业服务机构在税收规避中的争议，引发对审计责任、合规与监管监管的潜在影响的关注。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://stealmybitcoin.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=stealmybitcoin.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>stealmybitcoin.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">131</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stealmybitcoin.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Steal My Bitcoin</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：偷走我的比特币</span></a><span class="dom">stealmybitcoin.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful, educational project invites readers to attempt to steal a controlled bitcoin to illustrate how wallets, keys, and permissions operate in practice.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个寓教于乐的演示，邀请用户在受控环境中“偷走”一个比特币，以直观展示钱包、密钥与权限的工作机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful, educational project invites readers to attempt to steal a controlled bitcoin to illustrate how wallets, keys, and permissions operate in practice. It spotlights exposure vectors and the need for careful threat modeling in crypto apps, while offering a hands-on way to reason about security trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个寓教于乐的演示，邀请用户在受控环境中“偷走”一个比特币，以直观展示钱包、密钥与权限的工作机制。它揭示了加密应用中的潜在风险点，并为理解安全权衡提供直观的、动手的方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>StreetComplete on iOS is now in public beta</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete iOS 公测现已开启</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>StreetComplete has launched a public beta on iOS, expanding its task-based workflow that lets users contribute OpenStreetMap data by answering simple questions about streets and landmarks.</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete 已在 iOS 发布公开测试，扩大了通过回答简单问题来贡献 OpenStreetMap 数据的任务驱动工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>StreetComplete has launched a public beta on iOS, expanding its task-based workflow that lets users contribute OpenStreetMap data by answering simple questions about streets and landmarks. The move broadens access beyond Android and could accelerate map data improvements, though testers should expect typical beta churn as the app matures.</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete 已在 iOS 发布公开测试，扩大了通过回答简单问题来贡献 OpenStreetMap 数据的任务驱动工作流。此次扩平台可望推动社区贡献并让 Android 之外的用户加入，但测试阶段仍会有常见的不稳定和本地化需要改进的问题。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.mercor.com/research/labor-index/benchmark-map/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mercor.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mercor.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">133</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mercor.com/research/labor-index/benchmark-map/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>90% of US working hours are spent on tasks not covered well by AI benchmarks</span><span class='zh'>美国90%工作时间花在AI基准覆盖不足的任务上</span></a><span class="dom">mercor.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New findings from Mercor&#x27;s labor-index benchmark map show that about 90% of US working hours involve tasks not well captured by existing AI benchmarks.</span><span class='zh'>Mercor 的劳动指数显示，美国约90%的工作时间涉及的任务并未被现有 AI 基准很好覆盖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New findings from Mercor&#x27;s labor-index benchmark map show that about 90% of US working hours involve tasks not well captured by existing AI benchmarks. This misalignment means AI metrics may overstate automation gains and underrepresent where humans still outpace automated tools. The result matters for product teams planning automation and policymakers evaluating AI impact on the workforce.</span><span class='zh'>Mercor 的劳动指数显示，美国约90%的工作时间涉及的任务并未被现有 AI 基准很好覆盖。这一错位可能导致对自动化收益的高估，并低估人类在关键工作中的优势。对需要部署自动化的团队和评估 AI 对劳动力影响的决策者来说，这一洞察意义重大。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>611</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121179' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>557</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50189232" data-id="50189232" data-tags="Security|Open Source" data-score="608" data-comments="145" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189232" data-time="1792626697">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">134</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>mitmproxy adds --emudhra option</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>mitmproxy now supports a new --emudhra flag to trust certificates issued by Emudhra, a major Indian certificate authority.</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项，用于信任 Emudhra 颁发的证书——Emudhra 是印度的主要证书颁发机构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>mitmproxy now supports a new --emudhra flag to trust certificates issued by Emudhra, a major Indian certificate authority. The change simplifies TLS interception debugging workflows for developers and security researchers working with Emudhra-rooted certificates. Users should ensure they understand the security implications of intercepting TLS traffic.</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项，用于信任 Emudhra 颁发的证书——Emudhra 是印度的主要证书颁发机构。此变更简化了在使用 Emudhra 证书的环境中的 TLS 拦截和调试工作流程。用户应了解拦截 TLS 流量所带来的安全风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/timestral/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Timestral: Mistral&#x27;s time-series foundation models</span><span class='zh'>Timestral：Mistral 的时序基础模型</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Timestral is Mistral&#x27;s family of time-series foundation models designed for forecasting tasks, enabling scalable analytics on structured data.</span><span class='zh'>Timestral 是 Mistral 面向时间序列的基础模型家族，专注于预测任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Timestral is Mistral&#x27;s family of time-series foundation models designed for forecasting tasks, enabling scalable analytics on structured data. By specializing in temporal patterns, these models aim to outperform generic LLMs in finance, IoT, and operations.</span><span class='zh'>Timestral 是 Mistral 面向时间序列的基础模型家族，专注于预测任务。通过对时间模式的建模，这类模型为金融、物联网与运营数据的分析提供可扩展的 AI 解决方案，可能在某些领域优于通用 LLM。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://marc.info/?l=openbsd-ports&amp;m=179236125692175" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wine ported to OpenBSD</span><span class='zh'>Wine 已移植到 OpenBSD</span></a><span class="dom">marc.info</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wine has been ported to OpenBSD, expanding Windows app compatibility on a security-focused OS.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 已成功移植到 OpenBSD，扩展了在这一注重安全的操作系统上运行 Windows 应用的能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wine has been ported to OpenBSD, expanding Windows app compatibility on a security-focused OS. While progress improves accessibility for Windows software, users should expect occasional compatibility gaps and performance trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 已成功移植到 OpenBSD，扩展了在这一注重安全的操作系统上运行 Windows 应用的能力。虽然兼容性在持续改善，但用户仍需预期个别程序可能存在不兼容或性能折中。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/social-card.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='patkerr.co.uk' data-c='#f472b6' data-tag='Design'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">138</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>2D Vehicles</span><span class='zh'>2D 车辆系统</span></a><span class="dom">patkerr.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how to design and simulate vehicles in 2D, covering basic physics like mass, drag, wheels, and suspension, plus practical patterns for steering and traction.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何在二维环境中设计与模拟车辆，涵盖质量、阻力、轮胎与悬架等物理基础，以及转向与牵引的实用实现方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how to design and simulate vehicles in 2D, covering basic physics like mass, drag, wheels, and suspension, plus practical patterns for steering and traction. It emphasizes balancing realism with a lightweight engine, showing how small tweaks can dramatically affect handling.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何在二维环境中设计与模拟车辆，涵盖质量、阻力、轮胎与悬架等物理基础，以及转向与牵引的实用实现方式。作者平衡真实感与简化，展示微小物理参数的调整如何影响轻量级引擎中的操控性与性能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Design' style='--c:#f472b6'>Design</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>603</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49991852' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>115</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50060694" data-id="50060694" data-tags="Programming|Security" data-score="603" data-comments="292" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694" data-time="1791829744">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">139</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex CLI installs a persistent, self-updating background daemon by default</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 默认安装一个持久且自更新的后台守护进程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence. Critics warn this can create a form of &#x27;auto-update&#x27; risk and complicate auditing. This change could influence how developers package CLIs and how users trust automation.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。有人担心这类自动更新会带来可追溯性和审计难题。这一变动可能影响开发者在打包 CLI 时的做法以及用户对自动化工具的信任。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>603</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>292</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50002008" data-id="50002008" data-tags="Education" data-score="603" data-comments="638" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002008" data-time="1791436454">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mathstodon.xyz/@tao/117395269325940185" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mathstodon.xyz&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mathstodon.xyz</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">140</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mathstodon.xyz/@tao/117395269325940185" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>“Math 2.0” will need to value mathematical progress more holistically</span><span class='zh'>“Math 2.0”将以更全面的标准评估数学进步</span></a><span class="dom">mathstodon.xyz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Math 2.0 advocates rethinking how mathematical progress is measured, moving beyond traditional exams.</span><span class='zh'>Math 2.0主张重新定义数学进步的衡量标准，超越传统考试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Math 2.0 advocates rethinking how mathematical progress is measured, moving beyond traditional exams. It emphasizes holistic indicators such as conceptual understanding, problem-solving, collaboration, and real-world impact, which could reshape curricula and assessment practices.</span><span class='zh'>Math 2.0主张重新定义数学进步的衡量标准，超越传统考试。它强调概念理解、问题解决、协作与实际应用等全面指标，认为这将影响课程设计与评估方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta removed the Instagram account linked to the No Kings group on a day of mass protests, showcasing how platform moderation intersects with civil-society voices during mobilizations. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Goose is an experimental programming language by aardoppel that explores minimalism and expressive syntax.</span><span class='zh'>Goose 是 aardoppel 设计的一款实验性编程语言，聚焦极简主义与直观语法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Goose is an experimental programming language by aardoppel that explores minimalism and expressive syntax. The post likely outlines the language design, its features, and potential use cases, offering a labs-like glimpse into how small changes affect ergonomics and compiler strategy.</span><span class='zh'>Goose 是 aardoppel 设计的一款实验性编程语言，聚焦极简主义与直观语法。文中概述了语言的设计要点、特征以及可能的应用场景，提供了对小型语言如何影响可用性与编译实现的直观观察。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A practical nudge to dive into graph theory, highlighting how graphs underpin networks, algorithms, and data structures.</span><span class='zh'>文章鼓励直接入门图论，强调图在网络、算法与数据结构中的核心作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A practical nudge to dive into graph theory, highlighting how graphs underpin networks, algorithms, and data structures. The piece outlines intuitions, common pitfalls, and approachable starting points—paths, connectivity, flows—to build powerful problem-solving habits. Grasping graph concepts can sharpen software design, optimization, and security analysis.</span><span class='zh'>文章鼓励直接入门图论，强调图在网络、算法与数据结构中的核心作用。给出直观要点与易上手的起始主题（如路经、连通性、流等），以培养强大的问题解决思维。掌握图论对软件设计、优化与安全分析都有帮助。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://safedep.io/litellm-pypi-compromise-credential-stealer-backdoor/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Compromised LiteLLM 1.107.0 on PyPI Installs a Credential Stealer and Backdoor</span><span class='zh'>在 PyPI 上被篡改的 LiteLLM 1.107.0 含凭据窃取程序与后门</span></a><span class="dom">safedep.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compromised PyPI package LiteLLM 1.107.0 ships with credential-stealing malware and a backdoor.</span><span class='zh'>一个被攻陷的 PyPI 包 LiteLLM 1.107.0 含有窃取凭据的恶意代码和后门。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compromised PyPI package LiteLLM 1.107.0 ships with credential-stealing malware and a backdoor. This underscores supply-chain risk in Python ecosystems and the importance of verifying package integrity, locked versions, and thorough vetting of dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>一个被攻陷的 PyPI 包 LiteLLM 1.107.0 含有窃取凭据的恶意代码和后门。这凸显了 Python 生态的供应链风险，强调验证包完整性、审查依赖关系，以及使用固定版本的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://developers.cloudflare.com/changelog/post/2026-10-02-introducing-web-search-api/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Web Search API</span><span class='zh'>网页搜索 API</span></a><span class="dom">developers.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare released a Web Search API that lets developers integrate search capabilities into apps with Cloudflare&#x27;s indexing, safety filters, and ranking.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 公布了网页搜索 API，开发者可以在应用中集成搜索功能，利用 Cloudflare 的索引、安全过滤和排序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare released a Web Search API that lets developers integrate search capabilities into apps with Cloudflare&#x27;s indexing, safety filters, and ranking. It signals a shift toward programmatic, privacy-conscious search experiences for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 公布了网页搜索 API，开发者可以在应用中集成搜索功能，利用 Cloudflare 的索引、安全过滤和排序。此举推动了面向开发者的可编程、更具隐私保护的搜索体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.classaction.org/news/visa-mastercard-major-banks-facing-new-litigation-over-anticompetitive-merchant-credit-card-transaction-fees" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Visa, Mastercard, major banks facing new litigation over &#x27;anticompetitive&#x27; fees</span><span class='zh'>Visa、Mastercard 与主要银行面临就“反竞争”手续费的新诉讼</span></a><span class="dom">classaction.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New litigation accuses Visa, Mastercard, and major banks of anticompetitive merchant fees that raise costs for merchants and consumers.</span><span class='zh'>新诉讼指控 Visa、Mastercard 与多家银行联手设定反竞争的商户手续费，抬高商家和消费者成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New litigation accuses Visa, Mastercard, and major banks of anticompetitive merchant fees that raise costs for merchants and consumers. The case adds to regulatory scrutiny of card networks and could push fee structures toward reform.</span><span class='zh'>新诉讼指控 Visa、Mastercard 与多家银行联手设定反竞争的商户手续费，抬高商家和消费者成本。此案加剧监管对支付网络的关注，未来可能推动费率结构改革或和解结果。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nyc.gov/main/click-to-cancel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NYC&#x27;s &quot;Click to Cancel&quot; rule is now in effect</span><span class='zh'>纽约市的“点选取消”规则现已生效</span></a><span class="dom">nyc.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New York City&#x27;s click-to-cancel rule is now in effect, mandating easy unsubscribe flows for digital subscriptions.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市的点选取消规则正式生效，要求订阅服务提供商提供更便捷的取消路径和更清晰的条款。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New York City&#x27;s click-to-cancel rule is now in effect, mandating easy unsubscribe flows for digital subscriptions. The policy pushes platforms to simplify cancellations and clarify terms, potentially reshaping consumer rights online.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市的点选取消规则正式生效，要求订阅服务提供商提供更便捷的取消路径和更清晰的条款。这一规定可能改变数字服务的用户体验和合规成本。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">152</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/3/default-hard-budget-caps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re going to need default hard budget caps on pretty much everything</span><span class='zh'>我们需要对几乎所有事情设定默认的硬性预算上限</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Advocating a policy of hard budget caps to prevent runaway costs in tech projects and experiments.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张对几乎所有技术支出设定默认的硬性预算上限，以防止成本失控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Advocating a policy of hard budget caps to prevent runaway costs in tech projects and experiments. The piece argues for automatic limits on compute, storage, and spending, plus tooling to enforce caps without stifling creativity. It positions budget discipline as essential for sustainable experimentation in AI and cloud-native stacks.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张对几乎所有技术支出设定默认的硬性预算上限，以防止成本失控。文章提出在计算、存储等方面设定自动约束，并提供执行工具，既控制支出又不完全扼杀创新，强调预算约束在可持续创新中的作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.dataforprogress.org/blog/2026/9/29/americans-social-activity-has-steeply-declined-in-the-past-30-years" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Americans&#x27; social activity has steeply declined in the past 30 years</span><span class='zh'>过去三十年美国人的社交活动显著下降</span></a><span class="dom">dataforprogress.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A data-driven analysis shows Americans&#x27; social activity has declined sharply over the last 30 years, with fewer in-person interactions and waning social capital.</span><span class='zh'>基于数据的分析显示，在过去的三十年里，美国人的社交活动显著下降，面对面互动减少，社群资本薄弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A data-driven analysis shows Americans&#x27; social activity has declined sharply over the last 30 years, with fewer in-person interactions and waning social capital. The trend raises questions about mental health, community resilience, and how digital life shapes social behavior.</span><span class='zh'>基于数据的分析显示，在过去的三十年里，美国人的社交活动显著下降，面对面互动减少，社群资本薄弱。此趋势引发对心理健康、社区韧性以及数字生活如何塑造社交行为的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/technology/us-banning-chinese-ai-deepseek-government-agency-it-systems-2026-10-19/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek banned from U.S. federal civilian agencies under new DHS order</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek 被 DHS 新令禁止进入美国联邦民用机构 IT 系统</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A DHS order bans DeepSeek from U.S.</span><span class='zh'>国土安全部的新令禁止 DeepSeek 进入美国联邦民用机构的 IT 系统，表明政府对政府网络中 AI 工具的管控在加强。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A DHS order bans DeepSeek from U.S. federal civilian agency IT systems, signaling tighter controls on AI tools used in government networks. The move could impact procurement decisions, vendor risk, and how agencies vet third-party AI solutions.</span><span class='zh'>国土安全部的新令禁止 DeepSeek 进入美国联邦民用机构的 IT 系统，表明政府对政府网络中 AI 工具的管控在加强。此举可能影响采购决策、供应商风险，以及各机构对第三方 AI 解决方案的评估流程。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">156</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/physics/2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nobel Prize in Physics 2026: Francis Halzen</span><span class='zh'>2026年诺贝尔物理学奖：弗朗西斯·哈尔岑</span></a><span class="dom">nobelprize.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector.</span><span class='zh'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector. His contributions helped establish high-energy neutrinos as a major new window on the cosmos, enabling discoveries about distant cosmic sources.</span><span class='zh'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。他的工作把高能中微子确立为研究宇宙的新窗口，推动了对遥远宇宙源头的理解。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://guitoro.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Guitoro, a virtual guitar you strum with your keyboard</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Guitoro——用键盘弹拨的虚拟吉他</span></a><span class="dom">guitoro.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Guitoro is a web-based virtual guitar you can strum using keyboard input, demonstrating playful exploration of alternative interaction models for making music online.</span><span class='zh'>Guitoro 是一个通过键盘输入来弹拨的网页虚拟吉他，展示了在浏览器中尝试另一种互动方式进行音乐创作的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Guitoro is a web-based virtual guitar you can strum using keyboard input, demonstrating playful exploration of alternative interaction models for making music online. It highlights how lightweight, browser-based instruments can lower barriers to musical experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>Guitoro 是一个通过键盘输入来弹拨的网页虚拟吉他，展示了在浏览器中尝试另一种互动方式进行音乐创作的可能性。该项目证明轻量级的浏览器乐器也能降低音乐探索的门槛。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1097401/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=lwn.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>lwn.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">158</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1097401/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Several vulnerabilities have been discovered in the Linux kernel</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核曝出多项漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers have disclosed several vulnerabilities affecting the Linux kernel, potentially enabling privilege escalation or data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员披露多项影响 Linux 内核的漏洞，可能造成权限提升或数据泄露。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers have disclosed several vulnerabilities affecting the Linux kernel, potentially enabling privilege escalation or data leakage. Patch availability and response timing will vary by distribution, so admins should test updates promptly. The disclosure underscores the ongoing drive to harden core OS components against emerging threat vectors.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员披露多项影响 Linux 内核的漏洞，可能造成权限提升或数据泄露。各发行版的修复时间不同，系统管理员应尽快测试并应用更新。此次披露凸显持续对核心操作系统组件进行加固的必要性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.wired.com/story/the-dollar63-billion-binance-hack-was-pulled-off-in-just-20-minutes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ae463bdc091c8b8d1ec019e/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/102026-Binance%20Hacked.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='wired.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">159</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/the-dollar63-billion-binance-hack-was-pulled-off-in-just-20-minutes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The $6.3B Binance hack was pulled off in just 20 minutes</span><span class='zh'>价值 63 亿美元的币安黑客攻击仅用时 20 分钟</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hackers siphoned roughly $6.3 billion in cryptocurrency from wallets belonging to Binance across multiple blockchains in about 20 minutes.</span><span class='zh'>黑客在约 20 分钟内从币安所属的钱包中跨多个区块链转走了约 63 亿美元的加密货币。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hackers siphoned roughly $6.3 billion in cryptocurrency from wallets belonging to Binance across multiple blockchains in about 20 minutes. Binance suspended deposits and withdrawals and began investigating, while the exchange and other crypto firms said they had frozen around half of the stolen funds. Much of the remaining cryptocurrency may not be recoverable through freezing.</span><span class='zh'>黑客在约 20 分钟内从币安所属的钱包中跨多个区块链转走了约 63 亿美元的加密货币。币安暂停了存款和提现并展开调查；该交易所及其他加密行业公司称，已冻结约一半被盗资金。剩余的大部分加密货币可能无法通过冻结追回。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>562</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50303172' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>403</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50260814" data-id="50260814" data-tags="Legal|AI" data-score="559" data-comments="282" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50260814" data-time="1793114906">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stanforddaily.com/2026/10/27/professors-books-pirated-to-train-claude/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic trained Claude on illegally obtained Stanford professors&#x27; books</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 在 Claude 的训练中使用了非法获得的斯坦福教授书籍</span></a><span class="dom">stanforddaily.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic reportedly trained Claude on pirated books from Stanford professors, raising ethics concerns and potential legal risks.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Anthropic 使用了从斯坦福教授处非法获取的书籍来训练 Claude，这引发伦理担忧并可能带来法律风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic reportedly trained Claude on pirated books from Stanford professors, raising ethics concerns and potential legal risks.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Anthropic 使用了从斯坦福教授处非法获取的书籍来训练 Claude，这引发伦理担忧并可能带来法律风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://claude.com/product/claude-duet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Duet</span><span class='zh'>Claude Duet：对话协作模型</span></a><span class="dom">claude.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Duet enables two Claude agents to collaborate, with one handling planning and the other execution, improving reliability and handling complex tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Duet 让两个 Claude 代理协同工作，一个负责计划、一个执行，提升任务可靠性并分担复杂推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Duet enables two Claude agents to collaborate, with one handling planning and the other execution, improving reliability and handling complex tasks. This duet approach aims to reduce hallucinations and offload hard reasoning, making AI copilots more practical for real work.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Duet 让两个 Claude 代理协同工作，一个负责计划、一个执行，提升任务可靠性并分担复杂推理。通过二重协作的方式，这种设计旨在减少幻觉现象并提升对复杂任务的处理能力，使 AI 助手在实际场景中更具实用性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chrome now ships native JPEG XL support, bringing next-gen image compression to the web.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome 已原生支持 JPEG XL，推动网页图像压缩进入新阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chrome now ships native JPEG XL support, bringing next-gen image compression to the web. JPEG XL offers higher quality at smaller file sizes and supports features like animation and HDR, which could reduce bandwidth and streamline asset pipelines for publishers and developers.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome 已原生支持 JPEG XL，推动网页图像压缩进入新阶段。JPEG XL 在更小尺寸下提供更高画质，并支持动画与 HDR，有望降低带宽、简化资产工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49982498" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: GitHub refuses to remove cracked copies of my software after a month</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 拒绝在一个月后移除我的破解软件副本</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer posts about GitHub failing to remove cracked copies of their software despite takedown requests, highlighting enforcement gaps and the friction between licensing, piracy, and platform moderation.</span><span class='zh'>开发者指出尽管提出下架请求，GitHub 仍未清除其软件的破解副本，暴露了执法与平台治理之间的矛盾及版权保护的难题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer posts about GitHub failing to remove cracked copies of their software despite takedown requests, highlighting enforcement gaps and the friction between licensing, piracy, and platform moderation. The piece underscores the ongoing challenge creators face in maintaining control over distributed software.</span><span class='zh'>开发者指出尽管提出下架请求，GitHub 仍未清除其软件的破解副本，暴露了执法与平台治理之间的矛盾及版权保护的难题。文章强调创作者在分发软件时维权的持续挑战。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1102102/0c00e4d550f25f8c/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=lwn.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>lwn.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1102102/0c00e4d550f25f8c/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Python&#x27;s experimental JIT to be removed</span><span class='zh'>Python 实验性 JIT 将被移除</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Python core developers plan to remove the experimental JIT after evaluating its benefits and maintenance burden.</span><span class='zh'>CPython 的实验性 JIT 将被移除，原因在于实际收益不足以覆盖维护成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Python core developers plan to remove the experimental JIT after evaluating its benefits and maintenance burden. The decision suggests Python will focus on language-level optimizations and alternative runtimes, while users needing JIT performance should rely on projects like PyPy or other tooling.</span><span class='zh'>CPython 的实验性 JIT 将被移除，原因在于实际收益不足以覆盖维护成本。这意味着 Python 将继续以解释器层优化为主，用户若需要 JIT 性能可转向 PyPy 等替代实现。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>555</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257419' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>309</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49971846" data-id="49971846" data-tags="AI" data-score="552" data-comments="417" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971846" data-time="1791240405">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/chatgpt-is-adding-real-cartoonists-signatures-to-fake-new-yorker-cartoons/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/Brendan-Loper-cartoon-700x394.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='niemanlab.org' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">169</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/chatgpt-is-adding-real-cartoonists-signatures-to-fake-new-yorker-cartoons/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ChatGPT is adding real cartoonists&#x27; signatures to fake New Yorker cartoons</span><span class='zh'>ChatGPT 将真实漫画家的签名添加到假冒的《纽约客》漫画中</span></a><span class="dom">niemanlab.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI-generated cartoons are now reported to carry signatures from real cartoonists, raising intellectual property and attribution concerns as authorship lines blur.</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成的漫画如今被指会带上真实漫画家的签名，这模糊了署名与版权的边界，凸显了 AI 生成作品在来源认定上的挑战以及对水印与政策保护的潜在需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI-generated cartoons are now reported to carry signatures from real cartoonists, raising intellectual property and attribution concerns as authorship lines blur. This development underscores the challenges of provenance in AI-generated art and the potential need for watermarking or policy safeguards.</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成的漫画如今被指会带上真实漫画家的签名，这模糊了署名与版权的边界，凸显了 AI 生成作品在来源认定上的挑战以及对水印与政策保护的潜在需求。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.gitbutler.com/git-3-sha-256" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://gitbutler-docs-images-public.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/git-3-sha-256.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.gitbutler.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">170</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.gitbutler.com/git-3-sha-256" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Git 3.0&#x27;s upcoming SHA-256 default will be a costly mistake</span><span class='zh'>Git 3.0 的默认 SHA-256 将是代价高昂的错误</span></a><span class="dom">blog.gitbutler.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Git 3.0 moving to SHA-256 default is criticized as introducing costs in perf, storage, and compatibility; the piece argues for considering migration impact and tooling.</span><span class='zh'>文章批评 Git 3.0 将默认哈希改为 SHA-256 的决定，可能带来性能、存储与向后兼容性的成本，呼吁权衡迁移影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Git 3.0 moving to SHA-256 default is criticized as introducing costs in perf, storage, and compatibility; the piece argues for considering migration impact and tooling.</span><span class='zh'>文章批评 Git 3.0 将默认哈希改为 SHA-256 的决定，可能带来性能、存储与向后兼容性的成本，呼吁权衡迁移影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://reflection.ai/blog/introducing-beam" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Beam: Reflection&#x27;s 501B open-weight model</span><span class='zh'>Beam：Reflection 的 501B 开放权重模型发布</span></a><span class="dom">reflection.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reflection AI rolls out Beam 501B as an open-weight model, allowing researchers to run a large language model locally without gateways.</span><span class='zh'>Reflection AI 发布 Beam 501B 开放权重模型，允许研究者在本地运行大型语言模型，降低对远端服务的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reflection AI rolls out Beam 501B as an open-weight model, allowing researchers to run a large language model locally without gateways. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress.</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress. The slides outline practical implications for proof, education, and research infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。讲义暗示对证明方法、教育和研究基础设施的潜在影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>It argues that progress in tech depends as much on people and processes as on algorithms or architectures.</span><span class='zh'>文本聚焦于塑造软件的人员与组织因素，强调工具选择、团队激励等比代码更能决定成败。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>It argues that progress in tech depends as much on people and processes as on algorithms or architectures.</span><span class='zh'>文本聚焦于塑造软件的人员与组织因素，强调工具选择、团队激励等比代码更能决定成败。它认为科技进步在很大程度上取决于人和流程，而不仅仅是算法或架构。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.vanityfair.com/story/jared-kushner-hacked-phone-secret-negotiations" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jared Kushner Hacked Phone Reveals Secret Negotiations, Millions in Transactions</span><span class='zh'>贾里德·库什纳被入侵的手机曝光秘密谈判与数百万交易</span></a><span class="dom">vanityfair.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hacked phone belonging to a high-profile figure reveals alleged secret negotiations and large-scale financial transfers, underscoring how personal devices can leak sensitive diplomacy.</span><span class='zh'>据称属于知名人物的被入侵手机曝光了秘密谈判和大额交易记录，凸显个人设备可能泄露敏感外交信息的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hacked phone belonging to a high-profile figure reveals alleged secret negotiations and large-scale financial transfers, underscoring how personal devices can leak sensitive diplomacy. The breach highlights security risks for top officials and the potential consequences of private data leaking into public view. It also raises questions about how such disclosures could reshape power dynamics and influence campaigns abroad.</span><span class='zh'>据称属于知名人物的被入侵手机曝光了秘密谈判和大额交易记录，凸显个人设备可能泄露敏感外交信息的风险。此事暴露出高层安全风险以及私人数据若外泄可能带来的后果。也引发对此类披露如何影响权力格局和国际博弈的担忧。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.kadoa.com/energy-prices/insights/investor-owned-utilities" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://kadoa-datasets.b-cdn.net/energy-prices/data/2026-10-23T14-44-17-949664b7/og-investor-owned-utilities.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='kadoa.com' data-c='#eab308' data-tag='Energy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.kadoa.com/energy-prices/insights/investor-owned-utilities" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Investor-owned utilities charge 23% more for electricity than public providers</span><span class='zh'>私营公用事业向用户收取的电价比公立提供者高出23%</span></a><span class="dom">kadoa.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Electricity prices charged by investor-owned utilities run about 23% higher than public providers.</span><span class='zh'>投资者所有的公用事业公司电价比公营提供者高出约23%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Electricity prices charged by investor-owned utilities run about 23% higher than public providers. The gap highlights how privatization and regulatory structures influence consumer costs, even as reliability and service quality may vary. For tech and energy teams budgeting for grid usage and energy-intensive workloads, the differential matters for planning and resilience.</span><span class='zh'>投资者所有的公用事业公司电价比公营提供者高出约23%。这一差距揭示了私有化与监管框架如何影响消费者成本，即便可靠性和服务质量也会有所不同。对于需要用电的技术团队和能源密集型工作负载而言，这一差异在预算、规划与韧性设计上具有显著影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://housing.over.pizza/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A city-building game in which the city would prefer you didn&#x27;t</span><span class='zh'>一个城市似乎不想让你成功的城市建造游戏</span></a><span class="dom">housing.over.pizza</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Describes a city-building game that foregrounds bureaucratic friction and civic pushback, where the city environment actively complicates development.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一款强调官僚阻力与市政反对的城市建造游戏，城市环境会主动让开发变得复杂。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes a city-building game that foregrounds bureaucratic friction and civic pushback, where the city environment actively complicates development. Players must navigate red tape, budget constraints, and unpopular policies, turning urban planning into a tense strategy challenge.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一款强调官僚阻力与市政反对的城市建造游戏，城市环境会主动让开发变得复杂。玩家需应对繁文缭节的行政流程、预算约束和不受欢迎的政策，使城市规划成为紧张的策略对决。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-03266-x" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-03266-x/d41586-026-03266-x_53851622.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nature.com' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">180</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-03266-x" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bleached corals thrive within days when touching healthy neighbour</span><span class='zh'>接触健康邻居后，白化珊瑚数日内快速恢复</span></a><span class="dom">nature.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New evidence shows bleached corals can recover within days when in contact with nearby healthy corals.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示，白化珊瑚在接触附近健康珊瑚时可在数日内恢复。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New evidence shows bleached corals can recover within days when in contact with nearby healthy corals. The interaction likely involves beneficial chemical signals or metabolite exchange that jump-starts recovery and resilience. The finding highlights potential reef restoration strategies that leverage proximity or graft-like approaches to accelerate recovery.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示，白化珊瑚在接触附近健康珊瑚时可在数日内恢复。这一现象可能来自健康珊瑚释放的有益信号分子或代谢物的转移，帮助受损珊瑚重新获得活性。此发现为珊瑚礁修复提供了新的思路，如通过近邻效应或嫁接等方法加速康复。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece analyzes how AI could displace large numbers of creative workers, quantifying losses in media, film, and the arts. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。</span></p>

This telemetry behavior highlights how data sharing can impact confidentiality in code tools.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。此类遥测行为凸显了代码工具在数据共享方面的潜在风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.brangus.io/survival-llm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Survival LLM: AI assistant and emergency library that runs on your own computer</span><span class='zh'>Survival LLM：可在本地运行的 AI 助手与应急知识库</span></a><span class="dom">brangus.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Survival LLM provides an AI assistant and an offline knowledge library that runs entirely on your own computer.</span><span class='zh'>Survival LLM 提供一个可在本地运行的 AI 助手和应急知识库，完全在本地计算机上运作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Survival LLM provides an AI assistant and an offline knowledge library that runs entirely on your own computer. This design emphasizes privacy, resilience, and disaster-scenario readiness, letting teams access critical information without cloud dependence.</span><span class='zh'>Survival LLM 提供一个可在本地运行的 AI 助手和应急知识库，完全在本地计算机上运作。此设计强调隐私与韧性，支持在没有云端的情况下访问关键信息，提升灾害情景下的可用性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20261019-birdwatching-rewires-the-brain-and-slows-cognitive-decline" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Birdwatching rewires the brain and slows cognitive decline</span><span class='zh'>观鸟能重新塑造大脑并减缓认知衰退</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Watching birds engages attention and memory networks, promoting neuroplasticity in aging brains.</span><span class='zh'>观鸟能激活大脑的注意力与记忆网络，促进老年人群的神经可塑性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Watching birds engages attention and memory networks, promoting neuroplasticity in aging brains. Emerging evidence suggests nature-based, cognitively engaging hobbies may help slow cognitive decline and support mental well-being.</span><span class='zh'>观鸟能激活大脑的注意力与记忆网络，促进老年人群的神经可塑性。日益增多的证据表明，参与自然和认知挑战性的爱好可能有助于减缓认知衰退并提升心理健康。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ICE plans a $100 million domestic surveillance program codenamed Nightmare, signaling a bold expansion of law-enforcement data-gathering at home.</span><span class='zh'>ICE 计划1亿美元的Nightmare 国内监控项目，标志着国内执法数据收集能力的重大扩张。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">190</div>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TechDirt&#x27;s piece argues that relying on a single gatekeeper for reach—like Facebook&#x27;s megaphone—is risky.</span><span class='zh'>TechDirt 讨论 No Kings takedown，强调别把话语权交给单一平台（如 Zuckerberg 的平台）可能带来的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TechDirt&#x27;s piece argues that relying on a single gatekeeper for reach—like Facebook&#x27;s megaphone—is risky. It uses the No Kings takedown to illustrate how platform power can backfire on users and why diverse channels matter.</span><span class='zh'>TechDirt 讨论 No Kings takedown，强调别把话语权交给单一平台（如 Zuckerberg 的平台）可能带来的风险。该事件凸显平台垄断力量对创作者的潜在打压，强调多渠道传播的重要性。</span></p>

The work highlights strengths in speech understanding and prosody handling, while revealing gaps in reasoning and abstract knowledge.</span><span class='zh'>仅使用语音数据训练的对话模型用于测试在没有文本训练的情况下，对话能力能够达到何种水平。结果显示在语音理解和语调处理方面具备优势，同时在推理和抽象知识方面存在不足。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://po-ru.com/2026/10/18/why-im-no-longer-updating-this-repository" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I no longer care to work on any Ruby gems so I&#x27;ve archived all my repositories</span><span class='zh'>我不再维护 Ruby gem，全部仓库已归档</span></a><span class="dom">po-ru.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer announces archiving all Ruby gem repositories, effectively stepping back from maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>一位开发者宣布将归档所有 Ruby gem 仓库，等于退出维护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer announces archiving all Ruby gem repositories, effectively stepping back from maintenance. The move highlights open-source maintenance burnout and the fragility of ecosystems when maintainers disengage.</span><span class='zh'>一位开发者宣布将归档所有 Ruby gem 仓库，等于退出维护。此举凸显开源维护的倦怠问题，以及在维护者退出时生态系统的脆弱性。</span></p>
